## Best date plans

### 2026-11-28 — Beginners + Ayayay...

**Beginners, then a meal.** Contemporary wine-bar food at Ayayay.... It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12:15 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+West&destination=554+Vanderbilt+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/beginners/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/purchase/24875721/) · [Restaurant](https://www.instagram.com/ayayaynyc/)

### 2026-11-28 — The Last Waltz + Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Cafe

**The Last Waltz, then a meal.** Seasonal American at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Cafe. It is an 18-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+West&destination=Brooklyn+Botanic+Garden%2C+Palm+House+on+Lily+Pool+Terrace%3B+enter+at+990+Washington+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/the-last-waltz/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/purchase/24710565/) · [Restaurant](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/yellow-magnolia-cafe?date=2026-11-20&seats=2)

**Dinner, then Ruckus with Keir GoGwilt and Fiona Gillespie.** Asian-inspired bao and bowls at Hushh. It is 2.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Ruckus with Keir GoGwilt and Fiona Gillespie starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.

## What to do this week

### 2026-11-25

#### [Lightscape: Wednesday Off-Peak Night](https://www.bbg.org/lightscape)

Time to confirm · outdoors · $39 standard adult off-peak admission, including fees; member adult $33 · Brooklyn Botanic Garden · Crown Heights · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
A mile-long illuminated garden trail combining glowing trees, water features and seasonal light installations. Allow approximately 45–75 minutes.
**Timing:** Evening timed-entry event. Date verified; specific entry times were not exposed in the readable ticket listing. Choose a 15-minute window when booking.
**Practical note:** Use the Washington Avenue entrance. The paved trail is wheelchair accessible. Dinner at Lightscape Lodge requires a separate reservation and admission ticket.
**Why now:** The official calendar marks November 25 as an operating off-peak night; Thanksgiving itself is closed.
**On-sale watch:** A dated ticket and entry window are required; live availability was not verified.
[Official](https://www.bbg.org/lightscape) · [Tickets / booking](https://events.leapevents.com/tickets/lightscape2026/listing/)

Beginning with Gerard Malanga’s ANDY WARHOL (1964), in which the author of the SCREEN TESTS sits for his own extended portrait, this program finds the directors turning the camera on their fellow filmmakers, or even trading places for complementary films (as in the case of the Friedl vom Gröller and Christiana Perschon films that close the screening). ## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 25, 2026: neighborhood deals, November openings and Thanksgiving menus. Holiday exceptions and unconfirmed dates are flagged; reservations and tickets are not guaranteed.

Dining research: 2026-11-25T10:36:34+00:00

No Michelin awards are added. Amélie's website describes its Upper West Side menu as Bib Gourmand, but this review did not independently verify that designation with Michelin. Bib Gourmand, guide selection and Michelin stars are distinct; restaurant marketing alone does not establish a current award.

### Mercato Centrale's $1 morning drip coffee

Deals & happy hours — Mercato Centrale

A low-cost commuter stop offering La Colombe drip coffee alongside sandwiches and prepared foods.

Price: $1 drip coffee  
Schedule: Daily, 7–11am; market normally open 7am–11pm  
Conditions: Limit one coffee per person. Walk-in; no reservation needed. Additional purchase requirement, tax and service charges not stated. Holiday exceptions not posted on the checked contact page.  
[Source](https://www.centralenyc.com/mercato)

### Junoon's $43 two-course or $49 three-course lunch

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

The lunch Prestige menu provides vegetarian, chicken and seafood choices with included basmati rice and naan.

Price: $43 for two courses; $49 for three courses  
Schedule: Lunch; exact service days and hours unconfirmed  
Conditions: Menu lists $7 beside the lamb-chop option; confirm its supplement treatment. Published menu states a 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Tax and other gratuity treatment unknown. Reservations offered; requirement unknown. Holiday lunch availability unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### Amélie West Village's $1.50 oysters and $26 early-bird meals

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

Happy hour includes oysters, small plates and a burger or mussels with fries and a drink. Closed Thanksgiving Day.

Price: $1.50 oysters; $10 selected cocktails and small plates; $14 wine flights; $26 early-bird meal  
Schedule: Daily: drinks from opening until 6pm; food 4–6pm  
Conditions: $26 meal includes a beer or glass of wine. Oyster minimum not stated. Tax, gratuity and service charges not stated on this menu. Reservations offered; requirement unknown. Restaurant closed 2026-11-26.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/happy-hour)

### Amélie West Village's $26 weekday bistro lunch

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

A separately advertised lunch set with options including chicken paillard, croque monsieur and mussels. Unavailable Thanksgiving Day.

Price: $26 for two courses; optional wine of the day $10  
Schedule: Monday–Friday, noon–3:30pm  
Conditions: Tax and gratuity excluded. Complimentary bread basket; extra basket $3. Burger +$5; steak or Niçoise salad +$8; other additions priced separately. Menu displays three course categories but advertises two courses; permitted combination not explained. Reservations offered. Closed 2026-11-26.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/bistro)

### Amélie Upper West Side's $9 cocktails and $1.50 oysters

Deals & happy hours — Amélie Upper West Side

This branch offers a distinct happy-hour menu, including inexpensive small plates and extended oyster pricing on Sundays and Mondays.

Price: $9 selected cocktails; $11 Aperol spritz; $1.50 oysters; $14 wine flights; $26 early-bird meals  
Schedule: Daily, 4–6pm; $1.50 oysters also all day Sunday and Monday  
Conditions: Burger meal includes Kronenbourg draft; mussels meal includes house white wine. Oyster minimum, tax, gratuity and service charges not stated. Reservations offered. Official directory confirms Thanksgiving opening noon–8pm, but does not confirm happy-hour applicability during holiday service.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/uws/menus/happy-hour)

Deals & happy hours — Burger Village Fresh Meadows

The recently opened location advertises burger, chicken-sandwich or taco lunch combinations with a side and soda.

Price: $17 lunch special; draft beer $5 extra  
Schedule: Monday–Thursday, 11am–4pm  
Conditions: Not available on national holidays, including Thanksgiving on 2026-11-26. Burger/sandwich combinations include fries and soda; taco combinations include chips and soda. Tax, tip, service charges and reservation requirements not stated. Holiday restaurant hours unconfirmed.  
Opened: 2026-11-16  
[Source](https://www.burgervillage.com/fresh-meadows/)

Reservations offered. November 26 has separate Thanksgiving service; this lunch deal is not confirmed for that date. No end date stated.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/uws/menus/bistro)

### Amélie West Village's $25 November Beaujolais flight

Special menus — Amélie West Village

A seasonal three-wine flight featuring Fleurie, Morgon and Brouilly. These are selected vintage wines, not a claim that the flight contains Beaujolais Nouveau. Unavailable November 26 because the restaurant is closed.

Price: $25 for the three-wine flight  
Schedule: Advertised for all November; year and flight-service hours unstated  
Conditions: Pour sizes, food minimums, tax, gratuity and service charges are unstated. Reservations offered; mandatory booking is not stated. Closed November 26. The menu says 'All November' but does not print a year.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/beaujolais)

### Amélie West Village's $26 bottomless brunch mimosas

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

The brunch menu advertises a timed mimosa offer separate from food. Several brunch dishes, including eggs Benedict and French toast, are listed at $17.

Price: $26 for 90 minutes of bottomless mimosas; food priced separately  
Schedule: Saturday–Sunday brunch, 11:00 AM–3:30 PM  
Conditions: Ninety-minute limit. Whether an entrée purchase or whole-table participation is required is unstated. Tax, gratuity and service charges are unstated. Reservations offered; availability not checked. Official location page says closed November 26, which is outside the weekend brunch schedule.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/brunch)
